It doesn't... it doesn't give me anything back anymore. So I just don't bother.\n\nDoctor: Have these symptoms made it hard for you to keep up with your work or daily responsibilities?\n\nPatient: Yeah... I guess you could say that. Just... couldn't keep up.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel that losing your old apartment was what caused this depression to start?\n\nPatient: I... I don't know if it caused it.
